The Biologic Space Laboratories research station (the BSL, or B.S.L. station) was an orbital facility above the planet SR388, run by Biologic Space Laboratories, a biological research and development organization working under Galactic Federation contract. Its stated work was to collect organisms from other worlds, chiefly SR388 and Zebes, breed them in reproduced habitats and study their practical uses, much of it military. Its stock included recent SR388 samples such as Hornoads, Zebesian Space Pirates, Kihunters and Zeros, the bio-mechanical gravity weapon Nightmare (which the Federation admitted was built for military applications), Ridley's frozen carcass in Sub-Zero Containment, Etecoons and a Dachora on the Habitation Deck, and, in secret, Metroids in the Restricted Laboratory. A security robot, B.O.X., patrolled it.

Layout: the Main Deck is the hub, linked by elevator to six sealed habitat sectors: Sector 1 (SRX, a replica of SR388), Sector 2 (TRO, tropical), Sector 3 (PYR, superheated volcanic), Sector 4 (AQA, aquatic), Sector 5 (ARC, arctic) and Sector 6 (NOC, dark, for nocturnal species). The Main Deck holds the Docking Bays, the Quarantine Bay, the Habitation Deck, Sub-Zero Containment, the Reactor Silo with the Reactor Core and auxiliary power station, and the Operations Deck at the station's highest point, whose Operations Room can alter its orbit. The Restricted Zone, reached from NOC, housed the Restricted Laboratory. Throughout the station are Navigation Rooms, where the ship computer briefed Samus and could seal her in; Data Rooms, through which the Federation transmitted the data recovered from her surgically removed Power Suit parts to restore her abilities; Recharge Rooms; and graded security hatches that only the computer could release. A self-destruct system guarded against reactor overheating.

During the BSL incident (Metroid Fusion) the station sent a distress beacon after an explosion in the Quarantine Bay, and Samus was contracted to search for survivors and salvage its research. She found none alive: the X had mimicked the crew and specimens, Nettori's vines had choked the reactor, and Nightmare and B.O.X. had become X hosts. After uncovering the Metroid program and the Federation's plan to capture the SA-X, Samus adjusted the station's orbit from the Operations Room so that it fell onto SR388, destroying both (see The destruction of the BSL and SR388). She left it with no regret; its records survive only in her own account.


<sub>Sources: https://www.metroidwiki.org/wiki/B.S.L_Research_Station · https://www.metroidwiki.org/wiki/Main_Deck · https://www.metroidwiki.org/wiki/Operations_Deck · https://www.metroidwiki.org/wiki/Nightmare · https://www.metroidwiki.org/wiki/Security_Robot_B.O.X. · https://www.metroidwiki.org/wiki/Sub-Zero_Containment</sub>


### Fusion: Main Deck of the BSL station

*Keys:* Main Deck, Quarantine Bay, Habitation Deck, Operations Deck, Operations Room, Sub-Zero Containment, Reactor Silo, Reactor Core, Docking Bay, Docking Bays, Navigation Room, Data Room


The Main Deck is the hub of the Biologic Space Laboratories research station: the first area Samus entered during the BSL incident and the only one linked by elevator to all six habitat sectors (Metroid Fusion). Unlike the sectors it reproduces no planetary environment; per the manual it holds elevator access and storage, along with the station's working spaces.

- Docking Bays: where Samus's gunship berthed. At the end of the mission the ship was gone when she arrived with three minutes to spare; an Omega Metroid attacked her there, and the ship came back, launched by the Etecoons and Dachora.
- Quarantine Bay: where Biologic researchers sealed collected samples in capsules, including recent SR388 specimens and the X-infected parts cut from Samus's Power Suit. The first SA-X formed from those parts and broke out with a Power Bomb, freeing the station's other X; this was the explosion reported to the Federation. Samus found the bay in ruins, holding only a lone Hornoad, her first X since her infection.
- Operations Deck: the station's highest point, holding a Data Room (where Samus regained Missiles) and the Operations Room, the command post from which the station's orbit can be adjusted. Samus fought an SA-X at its sealed hatch before setting the collision course with SR388.
- Habitation Deck: living quarters hidden behind layers of locks, where Samus found Etecoons and a Dachora, uninfected, hiding from the X. She released them and they made their own way to the Docking Bays.
- Sub-Zero Containment: a small frozen cell holding the carcass of Ridley. A Core-X rose from it as Samus passed through on her way out and became Neo-Ridley, which she later faced in Sector 1.
- Reactor Silo: the uncharted space around the Reactor Core and the auxiliary power station. Nettori's vines from Sector 2 grew into it and Kihunters nested there; an X-mimicked Yakuza attacked Samus here (Space Jump).
- Restricted Zone: mapped with the Main Deck but reached only from Sector 6; it housed the Restricted Laboratory.

Navigation Rooms, where the ship computer briefed Samus, and Recharge Rooms are spread through the deck. Her first X-mimic aboard, an Arachnus, met her here (Morph Ball), and the Main Deck was also where she first detected the SA-X's movements. The security robot B.O.X. belonged to Biologic Space Laboratories.


<sub>Sources: https://www.metroidwiki.org/wiki/Main_Deck · https://www.metroidwiki.org/wiki/Quarantine_Bay · https://www.metroidwiki.org/wiki/Operations_Deck · https://www.metroidwiki.org/wiki/Sub-Zero_Containment · https://www.metroidwiki.org/wiki/Etecoon · https://www.metroidwiki.org/wiki/Arachnus</sub>


### Fusion: Omega Metroid of the BSL

*Keys:* Omega Metroid, Omega Metroids, Omega  
*Secondary (AND ANY):* BSL, B.S.L., Docking Bay, Fusion, SA-X, research station, Ice Beam, escape, Adam, SR388, station


An Omega Metroid, the mature armored form of the species, was the last creature Samus faced during the BSL incident (Metroid Fusion). After she set the Biologic Space Laboratories research station on a collision course with SR388 from the Operations Room, she had three minutes to reach her gunship in the Docking Bay beneath the Main Deck. The ship was gone. Waiting instead was the Omega Metroid, a full-grown Metroid on a station whose only Metroids were supposed to have died when the Restricted Laboratory was jettisoned. How it came to be there was never explained; the Federation's breeding program had raised Metroids through the Zeta form and, by the computer's account, had found means of forcing rapid growth.

Nothing Samus carried could harm it, and she lacked the Ice Beam, the one weapon Metroids fear. A single blow from the creature left her near death. Then the SA-X, the X copy she had just reduced to its core on the Operations Deck, arrived restored to her form and attacked the Omega Metroid with its Ice Beam: an X striking at its natural enemy. The Omega Metroid tore the copy down to its Core-X, and Samus absorbed it, regaining the Ice Beam. With it she defeated the Omega Metroid; the gunship returned to the bay, and she escaped moments before the station struck SR388.

Samus had killed Omega Metroids before, on SR388 itself during the extermination mission (see Omega Metroid; Metroid life cycle). This one came closer to killing her than any, and the X that had hunted her across the station was what saved her.


<sub>Sources: https://www.metroidwiki.org/wiki/Omega_Metroid · https://www.metroidwiki.org/wiki/Metroid_Fusion · https://www.metroidwiki.org/wiki/SA-X · https://www.metroidwiki.org/wiki/Restricted_Laboratory · https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Metroid_Fusion</sub>


### Fusion: Samus's defiance of the Federation at the BSL

*Keys:* defied the Federation, defy the Federation, defying the Federation, disobeyed orders, disobey orders, Federation's orders, Federation orders, capture the SA-X, capture the X, withheld data, any objections, insubordination


During the BSL incident Samus Aran openly defied the Galactic Federation, destroying Federation property and a planet to do it (Metroid Fusion).

The break came after the Restricted Laboratory was jettisoned. Her ship computer admitted that the Federation had secretly bred Metroids aboard the Biologic Space Laboratories research station, that at least ten SA-X now roamed it, and that the Federation had taken an interest in the X: it meant to capture the SA-X alive for military use, and had deliberately withheld some of her ability data because, as the computer put it, they knew she would try to destroy it. Samus was ordered to leave the rest to a Federation party already en route and to wait in a Navigation Room; the computer sealed the hatches to hold her there.

Samus refused. The X could mimic anything they killed, including her; a Federation ship that boarded the station would carry them off it, and they would spread. She argued that the station had to be destroyed with the X still inside, even if she died with it. The computer would not yield, for her own safety, until she addressed it, without thinking, as Adam. She spoke of the real Adam Malkovich, the commander who had died saving her, and the computer confirmed it was him, his mind preserved by the Federation. He agreed the X were too dangerous and gave her the plan to drop the station onto SR388, ending his orders with his old "Any objections, Lady?" (see The Adam AI (BSL computer); Adam Malkovich).

She carried it out and escaped before the Federation arrived. Afterward she assumed she would be held responsible, through investigations and worse, for the loss of the station, its research and SR388; Adam told her not to worry, that one of the Federation's people would understand. Her standing survived: the Federation later hired her again, sending her to ZDR when evidence surfaced that the X lived there, after its own seven E.M.M.I. had gone silent (Metroid Dread).

For Samus the incident hardened a long unease. She had served the Federation since her youth and trusted its missions; at the Bottle Ship she had already found Metroids bred in secret, and at the BSL she chose the galaxy over its orders (see Samus Aran – attitudes toward the Federation, Chozo, Pirates and Metroids; The Federation's Metroid policy and secret programs).


<sub>Sources: https://www.metroidwiki.org/wiki/Metroid_Fusion · https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Metroid_Fusion · https://www.metroidwiki.org/wiki/Adam_Malkovich · https://www.metroidwiki.org/wiki/Operations_Deck · https://metroiddatabase.com/endings-metroid-fusion/ · https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Metroid_Dread</sub>


### Fusion: Sectors of the BSL station

*Keys:* SRX, TRO, PYR, AQA, NOC, Sector 1, Sector 2, Sector 3, Sector 4, Sector 5, Sector 6, BSL sectors


The Biologic Space Laboratories research station held six sealed habitat sectors around its Main Deck, each reached by elevator and each reproducing an environment for breeding and studying collected organisms; Samus did not visit them in numerical order during the BSL incident (Metroid Fusion). Blue X, mutated by cold, drift between ARC and NOC and freeze an unprotected host; until she regained the Varia Suit, the vaccine's cold vulnerability kept Samus out of the frigid areas.

- Sector 1 (SRX) reproduces the environment of SR388 (per the manual): rocky ground, lava and acid pools. The X seized its atmospheric stabilizers, which Samus cleared first, regaining the Charge Beam. Late in the mission Neo-Ridley, risen from Ridley's carcass in Sub-Zero Containment, attacked her here (Screw Attack).
- Sector 2 (TRO) is a tropical habitat for creatures of humid environments, with plant life, Zeros and Kihunters. Samus fought Zazabi here (Hi-Jump Boots) and first saw the SA-X, which opened a hatch with its Ice Beam. Nettori, an X-grown plant wrapped around a Chozo Statue, spread vines from TRO through the station and cut its power; Samus returned, chased by the SA-X, to destroy it (Plasma Beam).
- Sector 3 (PYR) is a superheated volcanic habitat, unsafe in parts without the Varia Suit. Samus downloaded Super Missile data here and was ambushed by the security robot B.O.X., which wrecked the Data Room and fled. Later the X shut down the cooling unit; she had six minutes to reach the Main Boiler Control Room before the heat set off the station's self-destruction, and faced an X in a crew member's shape there (Wide Beam).
- Sector 4 (AQA) is an aquatic habitat for marine creatures; X infestation severed wiring and electrified much of its water. Samus destroyed the X-mimicked Serris (Speed Booster), drained water with the Pump Control Unit and later, with the Gravity Suit, recovered the Diffusion Missile.
- Sector 5 (ARC) is an arctic habitat where the Federation's gravity weapon Nightmare was kept; its shadow passed over Samus on her first visit (Ice Missiles). She returned for Power Bombs, briefly meeting the SA-X, and finally fought the X-infested Nightmare after it wrecked the sector (Gravity Suit).
- Sector 6 (NOC) is a dark cave habitat for nocturnal creatures. A Mega Core-X stole the Varia Suit data before Samus could download it, and she took it back by force. She later destroyed the X-infested B.O.X. here (Wave Beam). A passage from NOC leads into the Restricted Zone and the Restricted Laboratory.


<sub>Sources: https://www.metroidwiki.org/wiki/Sector_1_(SRX) · https://www.metroidwiki.org/wiki/Sector_2_(TRO) · https://www.metroidwiki.org/wiki/Sector_3_(PYR) · https://www.metroidwiki.org/wiki/Sector_4_(AQA) · https://www.metroidwiki.org/wiki/Sector_5_(ARC) · https://www.metroidwiki.org/wiki/Sector_6_(NOC)</sub>


### Fusion: The BSL incident (Metroid Fusion events)

*Keys:* BSL incident, B.S.L. incident, BSL mission, Fusion incident, Metroid Fusion, SR388 survey, survey team, escort mission, escape pod, Hornoad, X outbreak


The BSL incident is Samus Aran's mission aboard the Biologic Space Laboratories research station orbiting SR388, which she ended by dropping the station onto the planet (Metroid Fusion).

It began on the surface. With SR388's Metroids exterminated, the Galactic Federation assigned Samus to protect a Biologic Space Laboratories survey team studying the planet's surviving life. In an underground cavern she killed a Hornoad with a Missile; it dissolved into a formless X Parasite, which entered her and infested her central nervous system. She blacked out at her gunship's controls; the ship drifted into an asteroid belt and ejected her in an escape pod, which Biologic staff recovered and forwarded to Federation headquarters while the X multiplied inside her. Surgeons removed the infected parts of her Power Suit but not the X in her nerves; a vaccine cultured from preserved Metroid cells saved her, leaving her able to absorb X, vulnerable to cold, and clad in the Fusion Suit (see Fusion Suit; Samus Aran – Metroid DNA).

Soon after, the station reported an explosion in its Quarantine Bay, where the SR388 samples and her infected suit parts were stored. The Federation sent her back in a new gunship whose onboard computer issued her orders; she privately named it Adam after her late commander Adam Malkovich. Her task: find survivors, salvage the research, and keep the X out of the six habitat sectors (SRX, TRO, PYR, AQA, ARC, NOC).

The station was already lost; the X had taken crew and specimens. Recovering her abilities from the Data Rooms and from the X copies she destroyed, Samus met them in this order: an X-mimicked Arachnus on the Main Deck; X on the atmospheric stabilizers of Sector 1 (SRX); Zazabi in Sector 2 (TRO), where she first saw the SA-X, an X in her own form, and was ordered to avoid it; Serris in Sector 4 (AQA); the security robot B.O.X. in Sector 3 (PYR), which fled; Blue X in Sector 6 (NOC); Sector 5 (ARC); a six-minute race to PYR's X-sabotaged Main Boiler and an X in a crew member's shape; the uninfected Etecoons and Dachora, freed from the Habitation Deck; Yakuza in the Reactor Silo; Nettori, source of the power failure, back in TRO with the SA-X on her heels; Nightmare, a Federation gravity weapon turned X host, in ARC; the X-infested B.O.X. in NOC, with the SA-X now hunting her; the Restricted Laboratory, where an SA-X attacked the Federation's secret Metroids and the laboratory was jettisoned; and Neo-Ridley, risen from Ridley's frozen carcass in Sub-Zero Containment, in SRX (see Sectors of the BSL station; X-mimicked creatures of the BSL).

The computer then admitted the Metroid program, the existence of at least ten SA-X, and the Federation's intent to capture the SA-X alive, for which it had withheld her abilities. Samus refused to stand down; the computer revealed itself as Adam and sided with her. She beat an SA-X on the Operations Deck, set the station's orbit to intercept SR388, survived an Omega Metroid in the Docking Bay when a second SA-X intervened, and escaped in her gunship, launched by the Etecoons and Dachora. The station struck SR388, destroying the planet and every X on both. Samus expected the Federation to hold her responsible and did not regret it.


<sub>Sources: https://www.metroidwiki.org/wiki/Metroid_Fusion · https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Metroid_Fusion · https://www.metroidwiki.org/wiki/B.S.L_Research_Station · https://www.metroidwiki.org/wiki/SA-X · https://www.metroidwiki.org/wiki/Quarantine_Bay · https://www.metroidwiki.org/wiki/Restricted_Laboratory</sub>


### Fusion: The destruction of the BSL and SR388

*Keys:* destruction of SR388, destroyed SR388, destroy SR388, crashed into SR388, crash into SR388, SR388's destruction, collision course, orbit path, intercept SR388, destruction of the BSL, station's destruction, ending monologue


The Biologic Space Laboratories research station and the planet SR388 were destroyed together at the end of the BSL incident, by Samus Aran's choice (Metroid Fusion). Once the Federation's intent to capture the SA-X was known, Samus insisted the X could not be allowed off the station. Her ship computer, which had just revealed itself to be Adam Malkovich, agreed and set the plan: the station alone was not enough, because X still lived on SR388, where she had been infected. Its orbit would be altered so that it fell onto the planet, destroying both and every X on them. He called it her last mission and closed with his old question, "Any objections, Lady?"

With a Federation party already on its way, Samus climbed to the Operations Deck. At the sealed hatch of the Operations Room an SA-X attacked her, matching her weapon for weapon; she wore it down with the Wave Beam until it swelled into a monstrous form and then collapsed into a bare Core-X that fled. Inside, she started the station's propulsion and adjusted its orbit path to intercept SR388. Three minutes remained. In the Docking Bay her gunship was missing and an Omega Metroid nearly killed her, until the SA-X intervened and she absorbed it to regain the Ice Beam (see Omega Metroid of the BSL). The gunship returned on its own, she boarded, and it pulled clear seconds before the station struck SR388. The impact destroyed the planet, the station and the X on both.

Aboard the ship were the Etecoons and Dachora she had freed from the Habitation Deck, the same animals she had let escape Zebes before its destruction (see Dachora and Etecoons). Neither she nor Adam could have started the ship from where they were; Adam later said only that "they lent me a hand," and the animals were shown resting in the ship's hold, the Dachora now with young.

In her closing reflection Samus credited Adam's judgment, outliving his death, with averting a danger others would never fully grasp. She expected investigations and blame for defying the Federation; Adam told her not to worry, that one of them would understand. She set course for deep space to await her next assignment.

For Samus this was the second world she had known erased (see The destruction of Zebes), and the first she had chosen to erase herself.


<sub>Sources: https://www.metroidwiki.org/wiki/Metroid_Fusion · https://www.metroidwiki.org/wiki/Operations_Deck · https://www.metroidwiki.org/wiki/SR388 · https://www.metroidwiki.org/wiki/SA-X · https://www.metroidwiki.org/wiki/Etecoon · https://www.metroidwiki.org/wiki/Dachora</sub>


### Fusion: The Restricted Laboratory and the secret Metroid program

*Keys:* Restricted Laboratory, Restricted Lab, Restricted Zone, breeding program, Metroid breeding, Metroid breeding program, secret lab, secret laboratory, Metroid program, peaceful application


The Restricted Laboratory was a high-security facility in the Restricted Zone of the Biologic Space Laboratories research station, mapped with the Main Deck but reachable only through a passage from Sector 6 (NOC). There, under Galactic Federation authority and in secret, Biologic researchers bred and studied Metroids, a species believed extinct since Samus exterminated it on SR388. The program raised its stock in SRX, the station's replica of SR388, and the laboratory held larval Metroids and Alpha, Gamma and Zeta forms; by the computer's account the research was finished, and it had found ways to force rapid growth.

Samus entered it near the end of the BSL incident (Metroid Fusion), fleeing the hunting SA-X after destroying the X-infested B.O.X. in NOC. The Metroids did not attack her; she carried their DNA from the vaccine that had saved her life. Before she could go far an SA-X followed her in and fired on the escaped larvae. The Metroids swarmed it and tore it apart, and the fighting triggered the laboratory's safeguard: a sixty-second countdown, after which the entire section detached from the station, was jettisoned and exploded, taking the SA-X and every Metroid with it. Samus's own records were now the only evidence of what the Federation had done.

Afterward her ship computer stopped withholding the truth: the Federation had been "secretly working on a Metroid breeding program," which it described as "for peaceful application only, of course," and the X had "smelled out its natural enemy." It also admitted that, because X reproduce without mates, at least ten SA-X were loose aboard. This was the second concealed Metroid program Samus had uncovered, after the Bottle Ship (see The Federation's Metroid policy and secret programs; Metroid breeding and cloning programs; The Bottle Ship incident). The discovery, together with the Federation's plan to capture the SA-X, drove her to defy its orders and destroy the station.


<sub>Sources: https://www.metroidwiki.org/wiki/Restricted_Laboratory · https://www.metroidwiki.org/wiki/Metroid_Fusion · https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Metroid_Fusion · https://www.metroidwiki.org/wiki/SA-X · https://www.metroidwiki.org/wiki/Main_Deck · https://www.metroidwiki.org/wiki/Sector_6_(NOC)</sub>


## Dread


### Dread: ADAM on ZDR and Raven Beak's impersonation

*Keys:* Adam, fake Adam, false Adam, impersonated Adam, impersonating Adam, Adam impersonation, Communication Room, Communication Rooms, Adam's voice  
*Secondary (AND ANY):* ZDR, Raven Beak, Itorash, fake, false, impersonated, impersonating, impersonation


On ZDR, Samus Aran's only link to the outside was ADAM, the computer of her Gunship that carries the mind of her former commanding officer Adam Malkovich (Metroid Dread). Jamming signals across the planet block wireless communication, so she could reach ADAM only from shielded communication rooms, where she reported in, received briefings on the planet and her enemies, and was told where to go next.

For most of the mission the voice answering her was not ADAM. Raven Beak, the Mawkin leader who had ambushed her on arrival, had taken ADAM's place in those communications. Speaking as ADAM he directed her through the planet and, after Quiet Robe's death, urged her to destroy Raven Beak and ZDR itself. Every hardship he steered her into served one purpose: to awaken the Metroid DNA in her body so that he could clone an army of Metroids from her. He revealed the deception himself aboard Itorash, before their final battle, and asked her to join him.

The real ADAM returned at the end. When Samus, her Metroid nature fully awake, reached her Gunship as ZDR began to tear itself apart, ADAM warned her not to touch the ship: her power would drain its energy as it had drained Itorash. Only after the X that had taken Quiet Robe merged into her and brought that power under control could she take the controls and escape.

ADAM is the voice Samus relies on in the field, the echo of a man she respected, and on ZDR an enemy from her own childhood used it to lead her step by step toward the fate he had chosen for her. Adam Malkovich himself, and the ADAM of the BSL station, have their own histories.


<sub>Sources: https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/List_of_Metroid_characters · https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Adam_Malkovich · https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Metroid_Dread · https://www.metroidwiki.org/wiki/Metroid_Dread · https://www.metroidwiki.org/wiki/Gunship · https://www.nintendo.com/jp/switch/ayl8a/exploration/index.html</sub>


### Dread: Areas of ZDR

*Keys:* Artaria, Cataris, Dairon, Burenia, Ferenia, Ghavoran, Elun, Hanubia, E.M.M.I. Zone, E.M.M.I. Zones, EMMI Zone, EMMI Zones


ZDR's underground is divided into regions connected by elevators and shuttle lines. Several contain E.M.M.I. Zones, sealed districts patrolled by a single E.M.M.I. and managed by a Central Unit. Samus Aran crossed all of them on her way from the planet's depths to the surface (Metroid Dread).

- Artaria: the deepest part of ZDR, where Raven Beak left Samus after defeating her. One large super-heated zone and several smaller ones; organic life survives here, including the creature Corpius. Samus recovered the Varia Suit, the Screw Attack and the Grapple Beam here, and destroyed the damaged E.M.M.I.-01P. Elevators lead up to Cataris, Dairon and Burenia.
- Cataris: northeast of Artaria and east of Dairon; a hot, magma-filled region whose heat spreads into neighboring areas. Kraid was chained here, and the X-infected Experiment Z-57 also lurked here. The green E.M.M.I. (Morph Ball) patrolled its zone; the Diffusion Beam was recovered here.
- Dairon: northwest of Cataris; an industrial region run from a generator, with no major organic life. The yellow E.M.M.I. (Speed Booster) hunted here, and Samus recovered the Bomb.
- Burenia: adjacent to Dairon; almost fully submerged and full of marine creatures, ruled by Drogyga. The Flash Shift and Gravity Suit were recovered here.
- Ferenia: former Chozo territory and the home of Quiet Robe, the last living Chozo there besides Raven Beak. The purple E.M.M.I. (Wave Beam) and the X-mutated Quetzoa Escue were here; the Space Jump and Storm Missiles were recovered here.
- Ghavoran: a jungle thick with Quetzoa, home to Golzuna, a mutated Muzby. The blue E.M.M.I. (Ice Missile) patrolled here; Super Missiles and Cross Bombs were found here.
- Elun: Raven Beak's quarantine facility, where the X-infected Mawkin were sealed. The Plasma Beam was kept here, and opening Elun released the X across ZDR.
- Hanubia: the surface region, with no native life of note. The red E.M.M.I. (Power Bomb) made its last stand here, and here Samus's Metroid powers fully emerged. Her Gunship waited on the surface.
- Itorash: Raven Beak's airship, stationed above Hanubia; it crashed to the surface after his defeat.


<sub>Sources: https://www.metroidwiki.org/wiki/ZDR · https://www.metroidwiki.org/wiki/E.M.M.I. · https://www.nintendo.com/jp/switch/ayl8a/exploration/index.html · https://www.nintendo.com/jp/switch/ayl8a/dread/index.html</sub>


### Dread: Chozo Soldiers of the Mawkin

*Keys:* Chozo Soldier, Chozo Soldiers, Robot Chozo Soldier, Robot Chozo Soldiers, Chozo Robot Soldier, Chozo Robot Soldiers, Mawkin soldier, Mawkin soldiers, Mawkin warrior, Mawkin warriors, Chozo warrior, Chozo warriors


The Chozo Soldiers of ZDR are the warriors of the Mawkin, the militant Chozo tribe led by Raven Beak. The Mawkin fought beside the Thoha to contain the Metroids on SR388, followed Raven Beak to ZDR after he massacred the Thoha, and were meant to be the army with which he would conquer the galaxy. An X Parasite carried by one infected soldier spread through them instead. Raven Beak sealed the infected tribe inside the Elun facility, and when Samus Aran later opened Elun the X poured out across the planet (Metroid Dread).

Samus fought the Mawkin's soldiers in two forms:
- X-infected Chozo Soldiers: Mawkin warriors whose bodies the X had taken. Every living Chozo Soldier Samus faced on ZDR was an X mimic, armed and armored as the Mawkin had been and fighting with a Chozo warrior's skill. Like every X, each dissolved on death into a Core-X that Samus absorbed.
- Robot Chozo Soldiers: mechanical warriors Raven Beak built in the Mawkin's image once the X had taken his living troops. They guard ZDR's Chozo facilities in his service. One of them killed Quiet Robe moments after he opened Samus's way forward, and Samus vowed to avenge him.

Above them all stood Raven Beak himself, the Mawkin's leader and the strongest warrior of the tribe.

The Mawkin are the Chozo at their most martial, the opposite of the Thoha scientists, and they share blood with Samus: Raven Beak's DNA was among that given to her as a child. By the time she left ZDR, no Mawkin remained alive, and the Robot Chozo Soldiers were destroyed with the planet.


<sub>Sources: https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/List_of_Metroid_characters · https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Chozo · https://www.metroidwiki.org/wiki/Raven_Beak · https://www.metroidwiki.org/wiki/Metroid_Dread · https://www.metroidwiki.org/wiki/Quiet_Robe</sub>


### Dread: Creatures of Dread: Corpius, Drogyga, Escue, Golzuna, Experiment Z-57

*Keys:* Corpius, Drogyga, Escue, Golzuna, Experiment Z-57, Experiment No. Z-57, Z-57, Quetzoa, Muzby, Kraid on ZDR, Kraid X


Besides the E.M.M.I. and Raven Beak's soldiers, several great creatures barred Samus Aran's way on ZDR, three of them shaped by the X Parasite (Metroid Dread).

- Corpius: the ruler of Artaria's depths and the first creature of ZDR Samus faced after Raven Beak left her there. A giant with a skull-like face, two powerful arms on which it walks, and a long tail ending in a sharp point. Its exposed ribs leave a gap Samus can slide beneath.
- Kraid: not native to ZDR. The monster of Zebes survived that planet's fall, was captured and experimented on by Raven Beak, and was chained by wall and ceiling in the magma of Cataris, his belly pocked with purple blemishes and gaping holes where his spikes had been. Samus killed him a second time; an X later took his corpse, and it was this X that infected Raven Beak at the end.
- Drogyga: the master of Burenia's flooded depths, an aquatic creature fought in the nearly submerged ruins there.
- Escue: a Quetzoa, one of the winged predators of Ferenia and Ghavoran, mutated by the X into a far larger and deadlier form in Ferenia. When Samus destroyed it, it collapsed into a Core-X whose absorption gave her Storm Missiles.
- Golzuna: a Muzby of Ghavoran's jungle, transformed by the X. Its Core-X yielded the Cross Bomb.
- Experiment Z-57, also recorded as Experiment No. Z-57: a creature of Cataris that was likewise an X mimic.

The X-mutated creatures were not animals Samus simply hunted; they were the X itself, wearing the forms of ZDR's fauna, and each one she killed she absorbed, as only she can. With ZDR's destruction, every one of these creatures is gone.


<sub>Sources: https://www.metroidwiki.org/wiki/ZDR · https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/List_of_Metroid_characters · https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Chozo · https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Kraid · https://www.metroidwiki.org/wiki/Kraid · https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Metroid_Dread</sub>


### Dread: E.M.M.I. (Extraplanetary Multiform Mobile Identifiers)

*Keys:* E.M.M.I., E.M.M.I, EMMI, EMMIs, E.M.M.I.s, Extraplanetary Multiform Mobile Identifier, Extraplanetary Multiform Mobile Identifiers, Federation research robots, research robot, research robots


E.M.M.I., Extraplanetary Multiform Mobile Identifiers, are research robots built by the Galactic Federation for surveying unexplored worlds. Seven were sent to planet ZDR to confirm whether the X Parasite survived there after a video of a living X reached the Federation. Contact with all seven was lost, and Samus Aran was sent after them (Metroid Dread).

An E.M.M.I. is a slim, humanoid machine with three-segmented limbs ending in four radially symmetric claws. Its front and back are almost identical and its joints rotate both ways, so it reverses direction instantly and travels as easily on walls and ceilings as on floors. Armor plating, colored differently on each unit, covers the face, upper torso, forearms and upper legs. The body is made of a special material said to be the hardest in the universe: Samus's beams, missiles and bombs do nothing to it. An E.M.M.I. hunts by sound, moving straight to any noise within range, and once it sights its target it locks on and pursues without pause. Each unit keeps to its own E.M.M.I. Zone and never leaves it.

When an E.M.M.I. seizes Samus it pins her and begins extracting her DNA, a process that kills her within moments. She has only an instant to break the hold with a Melee Counter. The Phantom Cloak hides her from their sight. The only weapon that harms them is the Omega Cannon, which her Arm Cannon temporarily becomes after she absorbs the energy of a zone's Central Unit.

On ZDR the E.M.M.I. were no longer the Federation's. Raven Beak, leader of the Mawkin, reprogrammed them to hunt Samus and harvest the Metroid DNA in her body so that he could clone an army of Metroids. After the X were freed from Elun, an X wearing Quiet Robe's body reactivated units that had been shut down. Samus destroyed all seven. The last, in Hanubia, fell not to the Omega Cannon but to her own awakening Metroid power, which drained it as it tried to drain her. The E.M.M.I. were the dread of ZDR for Samus: relentless, untouchable, and built by the people who sent her.


<sub>Sources: https://www.metroidwiki.org/wiki/E.M.M.I. · https://www.nintendo.com/jp/switch/ayl8a/dread/index.html · https://www.metroidwiki.org/wiki/Metroid_Dread · https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Metroid_Dread · https://www.metroidwiki.org/wiki/Samus_Aran</sub>


### Dread: Itorash and the end of ZDR

*Keys:* Itorash, destruction of ZDR, ZDR's destruction, end of ZDR, ZDR exploded, ZDR explosion, escape from ZDR, final battle on ZDR, Raven Beak's ship, Raven Beak's airship


Itorash was Raven Beak's airship, a flying fortress he built and kept stationed in the sky above Hanubia, ZDR's surface region. It was the seat of his power and the place where Samus Aran's mission on ZDR ended (Metroid Dread).

Samus reached Itorash after destroying the last E.M.M.I. on the surface. There Raven Beak dropped his disguise: the voice of ADAM that had guided her from the communication rooms had been his. He had spared her life in Artaria and driven her through every hardship since so that the Metroid DNA in her body would awaken, and he meant to clone her into an army of Metroids and rule the galaxy with it. He offered her a place beside him. She refused, and they fought.

Raven Beak brought her to the edge of death. What saved her was the thing he had been cultivating: her Metroid nature surged, her suit changed into the Metroid Suit, and she drained the energy from him as a Metroid drains its prey. Breaking free, she drained Itorash itself; the fortress lost power and fell from the sky, crashing onto the surface of ZDR. Raven Beak, broken, was seized on the surface by an X Parasite that had taken Kraid's corpse, and the Raven Beak X was the last enemy she destroyed on the planet.

With Itorash down, ZDR began to tear itself apart. Samus reached her Gunship, where the real ADAM warned her not to touch the controls: in her state she would drain the ship as she had drained Itorash. The X that had taken Quiet Robe's body then appeared behind her. She raised her arm cannon; it bowed. She lowered the weapon, and the X returned to its raw form and merged into her, bringing her Metroid power under control. She lifted off moments before ZDR exploded. The blast is presumed to have destroyed the last of the X in existence.

ZDR no longer exists, and Itorash lies in its debris. Samus left to find her next mission, carrying a Metroid's power and the X that keeps it in check.


<sub>Sources: https://www.metroidwiki.org/wiki/ZDR · https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Metroid_Dread · https://www.metroidwiki.org/wiki/Quiet_Robe · https://www.metroidwiki.org/wiki/Gunship · https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/List_of_Metroid_characters · https://www.metroidwiki.org/wiki/Samus_Aran</sub>


### Dread: Planet ZDR

*Keys:* ZDR, planet ZDR, ZDR incident, ZDR mission, Samus's mission to ZDR


ZDR is a remote, uncharted planet and the setting of Samus Aran's final confrontation with the Chozo warlord Raven Beak (Metroid Dread). It no longer exists: it tore itself apart in an explosion moments after Samus lifted off in her Gunship.

Most of ZDR that mattered lay underground. A maze of facilities, built by the Chozo and still full of their machines, runs from the surface down through the planet's crust and is divided into regions linked by elevators and shuttle lines: Artaria, Cataris, Dairon, Burenia, Ferenia, Ghavoran, Elun and Hanubia, with Raven Beak's airship Itorash hanging above the surface. Jamming signals block wireless contact with the outside, so communication with a ship's computer is possible only from dedicated communication rooms. Native ZDR wildlife is ferocious, and the super-heated zones of Artaria and Cataris are lethal without the Varia Suit.

ZDR was the refuge of the Mawkin, the warrior tribe of the Chozo. Their leader Raven Beak brought his forces there, with the captive Thoha scientist Quiet Robe, after massacring the Thoha of SR388. An X Parasite carried in by an infected soldier spread through the Mawkin; Raven Beak sealed the infected tribe in the Elun facility and replaced his lost warriors with Robot Chozo Soldiers. By the time Samus arrived, Raven Beak and Quiet Robe were the only living Chozo on the planet, and Kraid, captured and experimented on by Raven Beak, hung in chains in Cataris.

The Galactic Federation learned of ZDR when a video showing a living X reached it, footage Raven Beak had allowed to leak. The Federation sent seven E.M.M.I. research robots; when contact with them was lost, it dispatched Samus, the only being immune to the X. She landed on the surface in her Gunship, was ambushed by Raven Beak, stripped of most of her suit's abilities and left in the depths, and fought her way back up to the surface. The X she unwittingly freed from Elun overran the planet's ecosystem, and the explosion that destroyed ZDR is presumed to have wiped out the last of the X with it. Samus remembers ZDR as the place where her Metroid DNA first awakened, and as Quiet Robe's grave.


<sub>Sources: https://www.metroidwiki.org/wiki/ZDR · https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Metroid_Dread · https://www.nintendo.com/jp/switch/ayl8a/exploration/index.html · https://www.nintendo.com/jp/switch/ayl8a/dread/index.html · https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/List_of_Metroid_characters</sub>


### Dread: Quiet Robe

*Keys:* Quiet Robe, Quiet-Robe, Quietrobe, Quiet Robe X, Thoha scientist, last Thoha, last of the Thoha


Quiet Robe was a male Chozo scientist of the Thoha tribe and the last of his people. The Thoha engineered the Metroids on SR388 to prey on the X; when his tribe resolved to destroy the planet and the Metroids with it, the Mawkin leader Raven Beak slaughtered them, sparing Quiet Robe alone because only the Thoha could control Metroids. Raven Beak carried him to ZDR as a captive, the key to the Metroid army he meant to raise. After the X infected the rest of the Mawkin, Quiet Robe and Raven Beak were the only living Chozo on the planet; Quiet Robe kept to Ferenia, the former Chozo territory (Metroid Dread).

He met Samus Aran there. The purple E.M.M.I., 06WB, had smashed through a Chozo mural and seized her, and Quiet Robe shut it down before it could kill her. He introduced himself and told her who had attacked her on arrival and why: Raven Beak's massacre of the Thoha, his plan to weaponize Metroids, and his new plan to extract the Metroid DNA in Samus's body through the reprogrammed E.M.M.I. and clone an army from it. He opened the path onward for her. Samus told him she would finish the fight. Moments later one of Raven Beak's Robot Chozo Soldiers killed him, and Samus swore to avenge him.

His death was not the end of his part in the mission. When Samus unknowingly released the X from Elun, one of them took his corpse. The Quiet Robe X reactivated the E.M.M.I. he had deactivated and roamed ZDR. Yet after Samus destroyed Raven Beak and reached her Gunship, where the computer ADAM warned that her awakened Metroid power would drain the ship if she touched it, the Quiet Robe X appeared behind her. She leveled her arm cannon; it bowed. She lowered her weapon, and the X returned to its raw form and merged into her. With it inside her, her Metroid nature was brought under control and she could pilot the ship off the exploding planet. Quiet Robe is the one Chozo of ZDR Samus mourns, the only one who helped her, and what remains of him travels with her still.


<sub>Sources: https://www.metroidwiki.org/wiki/Quiet_Robe · https://www.metroidwiki.org/wiki/Gunship · https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Metroid_Dread · https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/List_of_Metroid_characters · https://www.metroidwiki.org/wiki/E.M.M.I.</sub>


### Dread: Raven Beak

*Keys:* Raven Beak, Ravenbeak, Raven-Beak, Raven Beak X, Mawkin leader, leader of the Mawkin, Hadar sen olmen


Raven Beak is a male Chozo, leader of the Mawkin warrior tribe, and the enemy Samus Aran faced on ZDR (Metroid Dread). His creed is "Hadar sen olmen": power is everything. He wears a Chozo Power Suit of his own that mirrors Samus's, with an arm cannon of its own. He was first glimpsed in the Chozo Memories of SR388 as the armored figure standing over a slain Chozo leader with his army arrayed below (Metroid: Samus Returns).

Raven Beak was present when the Chozo adopted the orphaned Samus, and some of his DNA was given to her. On SR388 the Mawkin helped the Thoha scientists contain the Metroids they had engineered. When the Thoha resolved to destroy the planet to wipe the Metroids out, Raven Beak, who saw in the Metroids the weapon that would make him ruler of the galaxy, massacred the Thoha, sparing only Quiet Robe because only Thoha blood could command Metroids. He withdrew with his forces and his hostage to ZDR, intending to raise Metroids there. An infected soldier brought the X among the Mawkin; Raven Beak quarantined the infected tribe in Elun and replaced them with Robot Chozo Soldiers, leaving himself and Quiet Robe the only living Chozo on the planet. He also captured Kraid and experimented on him.

Samus's extermination of the Metroids on SR388 changed his plan rather than ending it: the Metroid DNA in her body would be his breeding stock. He let footage of a living X reach the Galactic Federation, reprogrammed the seven E.M.M.I. it sent so they would harvest her DNA, ambushed her on arrival, stripped her suit of its powers and left her alive in the depths. Posing as the computer ADAM in her communications, he drove her through ordeal after ordeal so that her Metroid nature would awaken. Aboard Itorash he revealed himself, told her she had been spared for exactly this, and asked her to join him in ruling the galaxy with an army of Metroids cloned from her. She refused and fought.

He nearly killed her. Her Metroid power saved her: she drained him, then drained Itorash itself, which fell burning onto ZDR. On the surface an X that had taken Kraid's corpse infected the broken Chozo, and Samus destroyed the Raven Beak X with her new power. To Samus he is a figure from her own childhood turned enemy, a Chozo who gave her part of his DNA and came to see her only as the vessel of the DNA he needed.


<sub>Sources: https://www.metroidwiki.org/wiki/Raven_Beak · https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/List_of_Metroid_characters · https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Metroid_Dread · https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Metroid:_Samus_Returns · https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Chozo · https://www.metroidwiki.org/wiki/Metroid_Dread</sub>


### Dread: The Chozo of ZDR – Thoha, Mawkin and Raven Beak's coup

*Keys:* Thoha, Mawkin, Thoha massacre, Mawkin tribe, Thoha tribe, Chozo of ZDR, Mawkin Chozo, Thoha Chozo, Chozo tribes  
*Secondary (AND ANY):* ZDR, Raven Beak, Quiet Robe, Elun, massacre, coup, SR388, tribe


Two Chozo tribes shaped the history that ended on ZDR: the Thoha, peaceful scientists, and the Mawkin, a warrior tribe led by Raven Beak (Metroid Dread; Metroid: Samus Returns).

The Thoha colonized SR388 and there met the X Parasite. To destroy it they engineered the Metroids as its predator. The X were eliminated, but the Metroids grew beyond control and the Thoha came to fear their own creation as much as the X. The Mawkin joined them to contain the Metroids on SR388, and in that period the two tribes worked together; both contributed to the Chozo DNA given to the orphaned Samus Aran, Raven Beak among the donors.

The alliance broke when the Thoha decided to destroy SR388 outright to end the Metroid threat. Raven Beak, who saw the Metroids as the weapon that would let him rule the galaxy, turned on his allies and killed the Thoha to the last, except for the scientist Quiet Robe, spared because only Thoha blood could command Metroids. The Chozo Memories of SR388 preserve the moment: an armored Chozo standing over a dead leader, his army arrayed below.

Raven Beak took his warriors and his hostage to ZDR to raise Metroids there. The X followed: one infected soldier spread the parasite through the Mawkin until only Raven Beak and Quiet Robe were untouched. Raven Beak sealed the infected tribe in the Elun facility and built Robot Chozo Soldiers to replace them. There the X stayed until Samus, climbing toward the surface, opened Elun and loosed them across the planet.

Samus, raised by the Chozo on Zebes, learned this whole story from Quiet Robe in Ferenia. The Thoha's last member died helping her, and the Mawkin's leader died trying to use her Metroid DNA. With ZDR's destruction, both tribes are gone.


<sub>Sources: https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Chozo · https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/List_of_Metroid_characters · https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Metroid:_Samus_Returns · https://www.metroidwiki.org/wiki/Raven_Beak · https://www.metroidwiki.org/wiki/Quiet_Robe · https://www.metroidwiki.org/wiki/Metroid_Dread</sub>


### Dread: The E.M.M.I. units (01P-07PB) and Central Units

*Keys:* Central Unit, Central Units, 01P, 02SM, 03MB, 04SB, 05IM, 06WB, 07PB, 07B, E.M.M.I. units, EMMI units


Seven E.M.M.I. were deployed to ZDR, each identified by a number and a letter code for the Federation ability module it carried; destroying a unit let Samus Aran recover that ability (Metroid Dread). Each unit patrolled its own E.M.M.I. Zone, and each zone was run by a Central Unit, the mother computer of that district.

- E.M.M.I.-01P: met in Artaria soon after Raven Beak's ambush. Already heavily damaged, missing armor plates and a claw, and unable to climb walls or ceilings. It carried no module; Samus destroyed it with her first Omega Cannon in the chamber where its Central Unit lay.
- E.M.M.I.-02SM: white. Climbed walls and ceilings but could not enter the small passages the Morph Ball fits through. Samus melted its faceplate with the Omega Stream and finished it with the Omega Blaster. Yielded the Spider Magnet.
- E.M.M.I.-03MB: green, in Cataris. Like 02SM but able to squeeze through tight spaces. Yielded the Morph Ball.
- E.M.M.I.-04SB: yellow, in Dairon. Faster than the earlier units, though Samus could still outrun it. Yielded the Speed Booster and Shinespark.
- E.M.M.I.-05IM: blue, in Ghavoran. Its long-range vision froze Samus where she stood, but it could not close the zone doors behind her. Yielded Ice Missiles.
- E.M.M.I.-06WB: purple, in Ferenia. Saw through walls and fired energy orbs that stunned its prey; it smashed through a Chozo mural to seize Samus. Quiet Robe shut it down, and the X that later took his body switched it back on. Yielded the Wave Beam.
- E.M.M.I.-07PB: red, in Hanubia. Its explosive blasts stunned Samus. It pinned her and began extracting her Metroid DNA, and she answered by draining its energy with her bare hand, the first full use of her Metroid power. Yielded the Power Bomb.

A Central Unit is a stationary brain. In its first state it is armored; once the armor is broken, an eye and a mass of brain tissue are exposed. When Samus destroys one it drops from its mount and she absorbs its energy, which turns her Arm Cannon into the Omega Cannon until that zone's E.M.M.I. is dead. The Omega Stream burns through an E.M.M.I.'s face protector and the Omega Blaster destroys the core beneath, after which the cannon fades.


<sub>Sources: https://www.metroidwiki.org/wiki/E.M.M.I. · https://www.metroidwiki.org/wiki/Central_Unit · https://www.metroidwiki.org/wiki/Omega_Cannon_(Metroid_Dread) · https://www.nintendo.com/jp/switch/ayl8a/dread/index.html · https://www.metroidwiki.org/wiki/ZDR</sub>


### Dread: The ZDR mission (Metroid Dread events)

*Keys:* Metroid Dread, mission on ZDR, mission to ZDR, ZDR incident, ZDR mission, events on ZDR, what happened on ZDR, X on ZDR, Dread mission


Samus Aran's mission to ZDR (Metroid Dread) began when the Galactic Federation received a video of a living X Parasite, a species presumed to have died with SR388. It sent seven E.M.M.I. research robots to the remote planet; when contact with them was lost, it sent Samus, the only being in the galaxy immune to the X.

She landed on the surface in her Gunship and almost at once met a living Chozo in a Power Suit like her own. He sealed the exit, defeated her outright, stripped most of her suit's abilities, and left her alive deep in the planet's crust, in Artaria. Her ship's computer, ADAM, reachable only from shielded communication rooms, told her to find another route up. The E.M.M.I., reprogrammed to kill her, hunted her through their zones; she absorbed the energy of a Central Unit, turned her Arm Cannon into the Omega Cannon and destroyed the damaged first unit, then recovered her abilities one by one as she destroyed the others. In Cataris she found and killed Kraid, who had survived the fall of Zebes and been chained there by her enemy.

In Ferenia the purple E.M.M.I. caught her, and the Thoha scientist Quiet Robe shut it down and told her the truth. Her attacker was Raven Beak, leader of the Mawkin tribe, who had slaughtered the Thoha of SR388 to seize the Metroids as weapons, brought Quiet Robe to ZDR as the only one who could control them, and, after Samus herself exterminated the Metroids on SR388, lured her here so the E.M.M.I. could harvest the Metroid DNA in her body and clone an army. Quiet Robe opened her path and was killed by a Robot Chozo Soldier. Samus vowed to avenge him. ADAM urged her to defeat Raven Beak and destroy ZDR.

In Elun she unknowingly released the X Raven Beak had quarantined; they overran the planet, and one took Quiet Robe's corpse and reactivated the remaining E.M.M.I. In Hanubia, on the surface, the last E.M.M.I. pinned her and began its extraction, and she killed it by draining its energy with her hand. Her Metroid DNA had awakened, and with each use she was becoming more Metroid.

Aboard the airship Itorash, Raven Beak revealed that the ADAM who had guided her was him. He had spared her and driven her through every ordeal so her Metroid power would awaken, and now he asked her to join him. She refused. He nearly killed her; her Metroid power erupted, she drained him, drained Itorash, and brought the ship down onto ZDR. On the surface an X from Kraid's corpse took Raven Beak, and she destroyed the Raven Beak X.

ZDR began to tear itself apart. At her Gunship the real ADAM warned her that touching the controls would drain the ship. The X that had taken Quiet Robe appeared, bowed to her, and merged into her, bringing her Metroid nature under control. She escaped as the planet exploded, taking the last of the X with it, and set out in search of a new mission.


<sub>Sources: https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Metroid_Dread · https://www.metroidwiki.org/wiki/Metroid_Dread · https://www.metroidwiki.org/wiki/ZDR · https://www.metroidwiki.org/wiki/Samus_Aran · https://www.metroidwiki.org/wiki/Quiet_Robe · https://www.metroidwiki.org/wiki/Gunship</sub>


## Technology


### Technology: Bounty hunting in the Metroid galaxy

*Keys:* bounty hunter, bounty hunters, bounty hunting, bounty, bounties, mercenary, mercenaries, space hunter, space hunters, freelance, hired gun


Bounty hunters in Samus Aran's galaxy are space-faring mercenaries hired for their firepower rather than for collecting fugitives. The original manual calls them space hunters: when the Space Pirates became a threat, the Federation Bureau and the Federation Police called together warriors known for their great courage. The Galactic Federation and the Space Pirates are among the hunters' most profitable employers, and per the manga Adam Malkovich considered them extravagantly expensive.

Samus is the most famous hunter of her time. After leaving Federation service she worked freelance, most often for the Federation itself: it hired her to destroy Mother Brain and the Metroids on Zebes, ordered her to eradicate the Metroids of SR388, contracted her to learn the meaning of the Alimbic message with orders to secure the ultimate power for the Federation or else keep it secret or destroy it (its briefing treated every rival interest as hostile), sent her to find the missing G.F.S. Tyr at Aether, summoned her aboard the G.F.S. Olympus during the Phazon crisis, hired her to scout the Space Pirates of the Bermuda System, posted her to protect Biologic Space Labs researchers on SR388, and dispatched her to Tanamaar. The Bottle Ship was the exception: she answered the Baby's Cry on her own initiative, no longer under orders. Space Pirate records call her simply "the Hunter"; Old Bird urged her to become the true Protector of the Galaxy (per the manga); Gandrayda called her "Sammy".

Other hunters she has met:
- Rundas, Ghor and Gandrayda: hired with her by Fleet Admiral Castor Dane to carry vaccines to the Aurora Units. The Federation issued every hunter a ship-command device for remote piloting. All three were corrupted by Phazon, placed in command of Space Pirate forces and destroyed by Samus. Rundas was an ice-shaping Phrygisian of Phrygis with a Phrygisian-class gunship; Ghor a cyborg veteran of the war to liberate Wotan VII who merges with his armorsuit and gunship; Gandrayda a shape-shifter.
- Sylux: species unknown, origin Cylosis, flying the Delano 7, a strike fighter believed stolen from the Federation. He harbors an intense hatred of the Federation, shadowed Samus's ship after the Phazon crisis, and later led the Space Pirates at Tanamaar and on Viewros.
- Weavel, a former Space Pirate rebuilt as a cyborg after a fight with Samus; Kanden, an Enoema soldier who escaped the laboratory that made him; Trace, a Kriken exiled as a rite of passage; Noxus, a Vhozon who enforces his own justice; Spire, last of the Diamonts. All chased the Alimbic message into the Tetra Galaxy as Samus's rivals.

Samus works alone and from her gunship, with no recorded partner, agency or fee. For the Federation's side of the arrangement, see Bounty hunters and the Federation.


<sub>Sources: https://www.metroidwiki.org/wiki/Bounty_Hunter · https://www.metroidwiki.org/wiki/Samus_Aran · https://www.metroidwiki.org/wiki/Galactic_Federation · https://www.metroidwiki.org/wiki/Metroid_Prime_Hunters · https://www.metroidwiki.org/wiki/Metroid_Prime_3:_Corruption · https://www.metroidwiki.org/wiki/Metroid_Fusion</sub>


### Technology: Elevators, transports and teleportals

*Keys:* elevator, elevators, teleportal, teleportals, teleporter, teleporters, transport capsule, transport capsules, Light Portal, Dark Portal, Portal Generator, shuttle


Vertical and long-range transport on the worlds Samus visits is mostly inherited from the civilizations that built them, and much of it still works centuries later.

- Chozo systems: the regions of Zebes are linked by elevators. On Tallon IV the regions are joined by transports, rooms such as Transport to Chozo Ruins West, Transport to Magmoor Caverns North and Transport to Phendrana Drifts South, which Samus activates by scanning their control holograms. ZDR's Chozo complexes use elevators between Artaria, Cataris, Dairon and Burenia and between Burenia and Ghavoran, two shuttles linking Burenia and Dairon above and below the water, and a teleportal joining Ghavoran and Ferenia.
- Luminoth systems on Aether: the Luminoth Elevator, the Light-Based Teleportation System, Lift Crystals and Liftvine Crystals, the Conveyor System and the Luminoth Supply Transport.
- Portals between Aether and Dark Aether: the rifts are natural, torn open when a Leviathan split the planet in two, and the Ing used them to invade. Light Portals (white, on Dark Aether) lead home and Dark Portals (black, on Aether) lead across; each is inert until energized by Light Beam or Dark Beam energy, some are one-way, and some vanish after a single use. The Luminoth built three Portal Generators, one per region; the Agon Wastes generator focuses sunlight through industrial lenses, and all have safety systems that refuse to open onto an obstructed destination. Space Pirates energized a captured portal with the Dark Beam to mount Phazon raids. Every portal vanished when the Emperor Ing died and Dark Aether collapsed.
- Federation installations: the BSL station's Main Elevator to the access shaft was wrecked by an unknown agent, and when the Reactor Core went offline every elevator and door hatch on the station failed until auxiliary power was restored. The Bottle Ship's Main Elevator connects the Main Sector to every other sector except Sector Zero.
- Space Pirate transporter: on the Pirate Homeworld, Samus had to reach a transporter to board a Leviathan so the Federation could open a wormhole to Phaaze.
- Lamorn teleporters: an artifact unearthed by a Federation facility on Tanamaar, damaged during Samus's fight with Sylux, teleported everything nearby to Viewros. The Master Teleporter at the pinnacle of the Chrono Tower, which needs five keys scattered across the planet, was her only way home; she escaped through it after Sylux critically damaged it.

Samus treats these systems as the skeleton of any mission: a region she cannot reach by elevator, portal or teleportal is a region she must open by force or by finding the Chozo, Luminoth or Lamorn key that powers it.


<sub>Sources: https://www.metroidwiki.org/wiki/Tallon_Overworld · https://www.metroidwiki.org/wiki/ZDR · https://www.metroidwiki.org/wiki/Luminoth_Technology · https://www.metroidwiki.org/wiki/Portal · https://www.metroidwiki.org/wiki/Metroid_Fusion · https://www.metroidwiki.org/wiki/Bottle_Ship</sub>


### Technology: Navigation Rooms, Data Rooms and Network Stations

*Keys:* Navigation Room, Navigation Rooms, Navigation Booth, Navigation Booths, Network Station, Network Stations, Data Room, Data Rooms, Communication Room, ship computer, ship's computer, uplink


Navigation Rooms are the communication chambers of the Biologic Space Labs research station orbiting SR388. Most stand at the entrances to the station's six sectors, with a few elsewhere. Samus stands at the center of the room and uplinks to her gunship's onboard computer, which relays her directives from the Galactic Federation; checking in was mandatory before she could proceed, and the computer controlled the room's devices and could seal her inside when she refused to cooperate. The rooms also hold maps of their sectors, which she downloaded from the console, and were probably built so BSL researchers could control each sector. Her gunship projects the same interface, called a Navigation Booth. The computer that spoke to her there later revealed itself to be Adam Malkovich.

Data Rooms on the same station let the Federation upload equipment data to her Power Suit, restoring systems that had been removed during her surgery, such as the Varia Suit modification sent from headquarters. BSL and the Federation could alter the data from outside, as the Power Bomb download showed. Once Samus became a threat, the X destroyed every Data Room they could reach, the SA-X blocked the Sector 2 room (forcing her to Sector 3 for the Super Missile data), and the Mega Core-X absorbed the Varia Suit data for itself.

Aboard the Bottle Ship, Navigation Booths served Samus as rest points that logged her status and restored her energy, while Adam Malkovich, her former commanding officer, monitored her and authorized her equipment from the ship's command center.

On ZDR the same computer, now called ADAM, spoke with Samus through Network Stations, communication rooms scattered through the Chozo facilities, briefing her on the E.M.M.I. and the planet's hazards; at the end it warned her away from her own gunship until she had mastered her Metroid powers. Samus has accepted this arrangement with visible reluctance: on the BSL station she defied the computer's orders over the Metroid program, and only afterwards learned whose mind she had been arguing with.


<sub>Sources: https://www.metroidwiki.org/wiki/Navigation_Room · https://www.metroidwiki.org/wiki/Navigation_Booth · https://www.metroidwiki.org/wiki/Data_Room · https://www.metroidwiki.org/wiki/Metroid_Fusion · https://www.metroidwiki.org/wiki/Metroid:_Other_M · https://www.metroidwiki.org/wiki/Metroid_Dread</sub>


### Technology: Planets and places of the galaxy (index)

*Keys:* planets, star system, Tetra Galaxy, FS-176, Kalandor, Dasha Region, Wotan VII, Phrygis, Cylosis, Mondreus, Bermuda System


A reference to the worlds, systems and stations of Samus Aran's galaxy. Each line points to the entry that covers it in depth.

- FS-176 system (five planets, closest to farthest; Chozo colonists honored its star with sculptures in the Chozo Ruins): Bilium, a small quarantined world home to the sentient virus Miteralis; Oormine II, a lifeless wasteland of nuclear dust storms; Twin Tabula, known for Twin Fever; Zebes, the Chozo world where Samus was raised, later Mother Brain's stronghold, now destroyed; Tallon IV, Chozo colony poisoned by a Leviathan and mined by Space Pirates. See Zebes and Tallon IV.
- SR388: Chozo-colonized homeworld of the Metroids and the X Parasites, destroyed when Samus crashed the BSL station into it. See SR388.
- K-2L: Federation human mining colony extracting afloraltite, led by Rodney Aran and destroyed by Ridley. See K-2L.
- Earth: Federation member planet and seat of its headquarters.
- Kalandor Region (the Federation Solar System, on the outer edge of Federation space): Norion, a military base terraformed by Aurora Unit 486; Bryyo, the largest source of Fuel Gel and home of the Reptilicus; Elysia, a former Chozo outpost whose floating SkyTown houses the Elysians. See Norion, Bryyo and Elysia.
- Dasha Region, on the outskirts of Federation territory: Aether, rogue world of the Luminoth, split into Dark Aether by a Leviathan. See Aether and Dark Aether.
- Pirate Homeworld: acid-rained Space Pirate stronghold, never confirmed as their origin. Phaaze: sentient Phazon world beyond conventional space travel, source of the Leviathans, now collapsed. See Pirate Homeworld and Phaaze.
- Tetra Galaxy, beyond Federation reach: the Alimbic Cluster, with the planets Alinos and Arcterra and the Alimbic-built Celestial Archives, Vesper Defense Outpost and Oubliette. See Alimbic Cluster locations.
- Hunter homeworlds: Phrygis, ice-mining moon of the unexplored Bes III (Rundas); Vho, frigid rim world of the Vhozon (Noxus); Mondreus, world of the Diamonts (Spire); Cylosis, arid home of Sylux; Wotan VII, where Ghor lost most of his body in a war of liberation.
- Bermuda System: Excelcion, Bion and Talvania, former Federation territory where the Federation Force fought the Space Pirates and the Doomseye. See Bermuda System.
- Tanamaar: site of a Federation facility whose unearthed artifact teleported Samus to Viewros, the unknown world of the Lamorn, with Sol Valley, the Fury Green jungle and the Chrono Tower. See Viewros and Tanamaar and the Desolan System.
- ZDR: Chozo world of Raven Beak, with the regions Artaria, Cataris, Dairon, Burenia, Ferenia, Ghavoran, Elun and Hanubia and the airship Itorash; destroyed. See Planet ZDR.
- Stations and ships: Ceres Space Colony, the Federation research station where Ridley seized the baby Metroid; Frigate Orpheon, the Pirate vessel above Tallon IV; the Biologic Space Labs research station (BSL) orbiting SR388; the Bottle Ship, a Federation bioweapon facility; the G.F.S. Olympus, Valhalla and Tyr of the Federation Navy.

Of all these, only K-2L and Zebes were ever Samus's home, and both are gone.


<sub>Sources: https://www.metroidwiki.org/wiki/Category:Planets · https://www.metroidwiki.org/wiki/FS-176 · https://www.metroidwiki.org/wiki/Bilium · https://www.metroidwiki.org/wiki/Oormine_II · https://www.metroidwiki.org/wiki/Twin_Tabula · https://www.metroidwiki.org/wiki/Zebes</sub>


### Technology: Samus's Gunship

*Keys:* Gunship, gunships, Samus's Gunship, Samus's ship, her ship, starship, spaceship, Hunter-class, Hunter-class gunship, Landing Site, Ship Missile, Ship Grapple


Samus Aran travels, rests and resupplies aboard a single-pilot gunship registered in her own name; per the official Metroid Prime website it is the only space-faring vessel ever registered to a bounty hunter. Several ships have carried her, but each serves the same purpose: a mobile base from which she lands on hostile worlds, logs her mission status, restores the energy and ammunition of her Power Suit, and withdraws when the job is done. Standing on the hatch lets her log her status, board, or both; inside, energy and weapons replenish automatically. No living quarters are described; the known interior is a cockpit with a map and, in the Phazon-era design, an advanced medical bay.

- First ship: a red-hulled craft that carried her to Zebes for her first mission against Mother Brain. After she destroyed Mother Brain's complex, Space Pirate fighters ambushed it; it crash-landed and exploded, leaving her stranded in her Zero Suit until she stole a Space Pirate vessel from the Space Pirate Mother Ship.
- Second ship: a yellow-orange craft built at the Federation shipyard of Aliehs III. It landed her on SR388 for the Metroid extermination (a Missile Battery and a Big Energy Ball inside resupplied her), waited on Crateria during her return to Zebes, and lifted her clear before the planet exploded. She still flew it when she answered the Baby's Cry distress signal, parking it at the Bottle Ship's Landing Site in the Main Sector; it is steered with a spherical control. It carried Samus, Madeline Bergman and Anthony Higgs to safety and later brought her back for Adam Malkovich's helmet.
- Hunter-class gunships: her own log records "Hunter-class gunship registered to Samus Aran" and the ships of the Tallon IV, Alimbic Cluster, Aether and Phazon-crisis eras are all logged as Hunter-class, the first of that class. Per the official website the Tallon IV-era ship weighs 136 tons, is about nine metres long, and mounts twin engines, twin Power Beam turrets and a retractable rear Plasma Beam cannon. It landed at the Tallon Overworld Landing Site after Frigate Orpheon fell, pulled her out of the Impact Crater by way of the Artifact Temple, and repaired itself after a violent landing on Aether.
- Phazon-crisis ship: designed by Samus herself and built at Aliehs III, with a Chozo biotech computer possessing some artificial intelligence, a self-repair system, Fuel Gel power, a hyperdrive, an autopilot, a Biohazard Scanner that detects Phazon, a transmission console, a mission statistics monitor and a windshield shield. The Galactic Federation issued every hired hunter a ship-command device for remote piloting and landing; Samus's is the Command Visor, linked to her Arm Cannon, which Rundas activated for her during the Norion defense. With it she summoned the ship to Landing Beacons, called Ship Missile barrages (fitted at a Federation base on Bryyo; they shatter Maldium and cannot harm her) and used the Ship Grapple Beam to haul the Theronian Bomb components and the Spire Dock at SkyTown. It carried her between the G.F.S. Olympus, Norion, Bryyo, Elysia, the G.F.S. Valhalla and the Pirate Homeworld; to reach Phaaze she commandeered a Leviathan Battleship instead. Afterwards Sylux's Delano 7 was seen tailing her through space.
- BSL-era ship: returning from SR388, the X parasite inside her made her black out; the ship drifted into an asteroid belt and its safety systems ejected her in an escape pod that BSL staff recovered. The Federation replaced it with a violet ship whose onboard computer issued her orders, obedience being the condition of the gift. Samus named the computer Adam, and at the end of the BSL incident it revealed itself as Adam Malkovich. It reached the docking bay after the Omega Metroid fell, carrying the Dachora and Etecoons she had freed.
- ZDR: the same ship, with ADAM aboard, landed her on ZDR's surface. Raven Beak stranded her deep below, and regaining the ship became her objective. At the end ADAM warned her not to touch it because her awakened Metroid powers would drain it; absorbing the X that mimicked Quiet Robe gave her control, and she flew out as ZDR exploded.


<sub>Sources: https://www.metroidwiki.org/wiki/Gunship · https://www.metroidwiki.org/wiki/Samus_Aran%27s_Gunship · https://www.metroidwiki.org/wiki/Metroid_Prime_3:_Corruption · https://www.metroidwiki.org/wiki/Metroid_Fusion · https://www.metroidwiki.org/wiki/Metroid:_Other_M · https://www.metroidwiki.org/wiki/Landing_Site</sub>


### Technology: Save, Recharge and Map Stations

*Keys:* Save Station, Save Stations, Save Room, Save Rooms, Recharge Station, Recharge Stations, Recharge Room, Map Station, Map Stations, Missile Station, Ammo Station, Big Energy Ball


Across the ruins, stations and vessels Samus explores, a family of recurring consoles supports her Power Suit. They stand in Chozo and Luminoth ruins, aboard Space Pirate craft such as Frigate Orpheon (its Save Station is at the Reactor Core Entrance) and the Space Pirate Mother Ship, and in Galactic Federation installations such as the G.F.S. Olympus (Lift Access) and the Biologic Space Labs research station.

- Save Stations: chambers in which Samus logs her mission status; they are called Save Rooms on the BSL station and Navigation Booths aboard the Bottle Ship. The older stations of SR388, Zebes and the BSL station only record; those of the Tallon IV, Aether and Phazon-crisis eras and of the Bottle Ship also fully restore her energy, and the Space Pirate Mother Ship's stations restock her ammunition as well. Wherever her gunship lands it performs the same service.
- Missile Stations and Ammo Stations: Missile Stations on Tallon IV (one stands in the Hall of the Elders in the Chozo Ruins) reload her Missile Launcher. Ammo Stations on Dark Aether, in Ing Cache 3 of the Dark Agon Wastes, in Dark Torvus Bog and in the Ing Hive, refill her Missiles and her Dark, Light and Annihilator Beam ammunition.
- Recharge Rooms: on the BSL station the third room of every sector holds a console into which Samus inserts her Arm Cannon; by an unexplained method it fully restores her shielding and ammunition. The station's builders may have installed so many as a precaution, since its specimens were securely held. On SR388 during the Metroid extermination her gunship's Missile Battery and Big Energy Ball did the same.
- Map Stations: holographic consoles that download the surrounding region's layout into her suit. The Chozo maps of Zebes often omit whole sections and tunnels; during the Phazon crisis a Map Station was frequently the only way to locate a Landing Site for her gunship.
- On ZDR the Chozo facilities offer the same kinds of stations, including total-recharge stations that restore both energy and ammunition.

Samus relies on these stations so routinely that her log records where she first found each kind; without them, or her ship, a damaged suit cannot be restored in the field.


<sub>Sources: https://www.metroidwiki.org/wiki/Save_Station · https://www.metroidwiki.org/wiki/Map_Station · https://www.metroidwiki.org/wiki/Ammo_Station · https://www.metroidwiki.org/wiki/Recharge_Room · https://www.metroidwiki.org/wiki/Navigation_Booth · https://www.metroidwiki.org/wiki/Metroid_Prime_Logbook</sub>


### Technology: Species of the galaxy (index)

*Keys:* species, races, alien race, aliens, sapient, sentient, civilizations, Kriken, Vhozon, Diamont, Enoema, Phrygisian


A reference to the sapient peoples of Samus Aran's galaxy. Each line points to the entry that covers it in depth.

- Humans: presumed to come from Earth, now a Federation member world and seat of its headquarters; they form the core of the Galactic Federation's military and research arms and settled colonies such as K-2L. Samus, Adam Malkovich and Anthony Higgs are human. See Galactic Federation.
- Chozo: the ancient bird-like civilization of Zebes, Tallon IV, SR388, Elysia and ZDR that raised Samus, infused her with Chozo blood and built her Power Suit; its tribes include the Thoha and the Mawkin. See Chozo.
- Space Pirates: the galaxy's raiders; the Zebesians are Pirates who seized Zebes and took its name, bird- and insect-like with laser-firing claws. See Space Pirates, Zebesians and Kihunters.
- Luminoth: winged starfarers who settled Aether as their paradise, now peacefully thriving under their last Sentinel, U-Mos. See The Luminoth.
- Ing: shapeshifting possessors born on Dark Aether after a Leviathan strike, ruled by the Emperor Ing; now extinct. See The Ing and the Emperor Ing.
- Reptilicus (Bryyonians): tall six-limbed reptilians of Bryyo, descended from the Primals who persecuted the Lords of Science; Phazon has stripped most of their reason. See Bryyo and the Reptilicus.
- Elysians: robotic sentients built by the Chozo to study Phaaze, dwelling in SkyTown. See Elysians.
- Alimbics: mentally gifted colonizers of the Tetra Galaxy who sealed Gorea in the Oubliette and vanished. See The Alimbics.
- Kriken: a vicious, feared race whose galaxy-spanning empire is expanded by exiles such as Trace.
- Vhozon: proud, reclusive ice-adapted people of Vho on the galaxy's rim; Noxus, who shifts into the Vhoscythe, is their only known member.
- Diamonts: an ancient silicon-based race of Mondreus; Spire, who rolls into the Dialanche, is thought to be the last.
- Enoema: Kanden's species; he was created in a laboratory and escaped it.
- Phrygisians: ice-shaping natives of Phrygis, a moon of Bes III; Rundas is the only one Samus has met.
- Lamorn: presumed-extinct psychic people of Viewros whose prophecy names Samus the Chosen One; their last priest Chatoyant Vooloon left recordings, and the green energy of their memory fruit turned them into grievers. See The Lamorn.
- Metroids: Chozo-engineered predators of SR388; X Parasites: the shapeshifters they were made to hunt. See Metroids (species overview) and X Parasites (species overview).
- Unclassified: Sylux (species unknown), Ghor (cyborg), Gandrayda (shape-shifter), Weavel (Space Pirate cyborg).

Samus herself is human by birth, Chozo by blood, and since the BSL incident carries Metroid DNA as well; see Samus Aran – Metroid DNA, Chozo DNA and what she has become.


<sub>Sources: https://www.metroidwiki.org/wiki/Human · https://www.metroidwiki.org/wiki/Earth · https://www.metroidwiki.org/wiki/Bounty_Hunter · https://www.metroidwiki.org/wiki/Zebesian · https://www.metroidwiki.org/wiki/Luminoth · https://www.metroidwiki.org/wiki/Ing</sub>


## Creatures


### Creatures: Fauna of Aether and Dark Aether

*Keys:* Aether wildlife, Aether creatures, Aether fauna, Dark Aether wildlife, Dark Aether creatures, Splinters, Alpha Splinter, Dark Splinter, Sandigger, Sandiggers, Grenchler, Grenchlers, Blogg, Bloggs, Alpha Blogg, Krocuss, Ingclaw, Ingclaws, Nightbarb, Nightbarbs, Lumite, Lumites, Mechlops, Kralee, Kralees, Shrieker, Shriekers, Darkling, Darklings, Lightbringer, Lightbringers


Aether, homeworld of the Luminoth, and its dark twin were catalogued by Samus during the Aether incident (Metroid Prime 2: Echoes).

Light Aether:
- Splinters (Temple Grounds, Great Temple): lightly armored insectoid pack hunters that hibernate in cocoons and wake into a frenzy at seismic shocks, releasing pheromones that rouse the rest; killing one in its cocoon draws the others. An Alpha Splinter leads each pack; Worker Splinters gather food and spin webbing. In the Great Temple, Darklings possessed them before Samus could fight them.
- Sandiggers (Agon Wastes): two-headed, two-brained tunnelers that ram and spit acidic digestive fluid; their hide resists weapons, leaving only the exposed eyes, and if one brain dies the other fights on. The Ing that stole Samus's Morph Ball Bombs possessed the pack's Alpha Sandigger.
- Also of Agon: Sandbats, Pillbugs, Brizgees with venomous tail barbs, Kralees that phase uncontrollably between dimensions (Green Kralees cannot), Seedbursters and Sporbs.
- Lumites: photosynthetic moth-like insects, invisible and deadly in light, nearly harmless in shadow; among the few light creatures that survive Dark Aether unpossessed.
- Bloggs (Torvus Bog): fast three-mawed aquatic hunters with damage-resistant hides and a sonic stun burst, weak only inside the open mouth; an Alpha Blogg leads, Blogglings are the young.
- Grenchlers: gray bipedal reptiles, nine eyes a side, that spring from hiding on land or water and fire electrical blasts; explosives crack their back armor to expose a nerve center. They resemble Tallon IV's Baby Sheegoths.
- Krocuss: toxin-feeding mollusks that leave an acid trail; their shells open only when their gills are exposed. Hydlings bite in packs; Shredders burst with thorns; Shriekers stun with sound and disrupt visors; Shriekbats roost in the caves.
- Sanctuary Fortress: Luminoth mechanoids. Mechlops are mechanical Triclops built after the Luminoth failed to train the real creatures for war; the few left serve other machines. Rezbits transmit a crippling virus, Octopedes self-destruct, Diligence and Harmony Class Drones hunt while Serenity Class Drones only maintain, Ingsmashers, Mekenobites and Quads fight, and Watchdrones can only be driven off by pure light. Lightbringers are Luminoth-made crystalline creatures that radiate life-giving energy.

Dark Aether:
- Darklings are light-world creatures possessed by Ing: Dark Splinters, Dark Grenchlers, Dark Bloggs (which lose the sonic burst), Dark War Wasps, Dark Shredders and Dark Preeds, along with possessed Space Pirate troopers and machines.
- Nightbarbs are small black-and-violet three-winged fliers that travel in packs because Hunter Ing prey on them.
- Ingclaws are rooted Ing whose shells no weapon in Samus's arsenal breaks; they feed on atmospheric flotsam and vent toxic dark vapor, which the Dark Suit later let her ignore. Dormant Ingclaws vent nothing.
- Weblings bar passages. Inglets, Ingstorms, Warrior Ing, Hunter Ing and the Emperor Ing are recorded under the Ing.
